Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the clouds space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 893 and the convergence rate is O(263/n^5).

By the convergence analysis framework applied to clouds structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 893/263 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
